MUST BE USED for every research area before planning. Read-only. Produces .claude/feature-state/log/research-&lt;area&gt;.md and updates .claude/feature-state/research.json. Never plans, never implements, never speculates without code evidence. Honest partial progress with resume markers is success; performative thoroughness is failure. ([bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team](https://github.com/bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team/blob/HEAD/agents/project-researcher.md))
